# Commands · R · part 3

1,487 entries, README to recall. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **README**: - `commands/` holds repeatable capsule-operator procedures. A command is a folder with a `README.md` that states the procedure and, where useful, machine-readable companion files. - Commands describe how to work on the capsule. They are not capsule protocol parts, are not loaded by any runtime, and must never be referenced from `capsule.json` `parts` or `runtime.json`. ([jehmal/IOS-TradeGuruElectrical](https://github.com/jehmal/IOS-TradeGuruElectrical/blob/HEAD/api/_lib/v1/tradeguru-capsule/commands/README.md))

- **README**: `rulesEngine.ts` is the command dispatcher: `resolveCommand(state, world, command)` `switch`es on `command.type` and delegates to a named handler that returns a `CommandResult` (`{ state, events }`). This directory holds cohesive groups of those handlers, pulled out of the dispatcher so the engine's core stays readable. ([kino-6/black-stela](https://github.com/kino-6/black-stela/blob/HEAD/src/domain/commands/README.md))

After a clean rebase, push --force-with-lease (or -u if no upstream). No MR creation, no Jira transition. Standalone — not wired into /run.
